실적 발표 후 주가 급락: 성장에도 불구하고 수익성에 대한 우려  
26.10.30 실적 발표 당일 기아 주가는 11.7% 하락했다. 하루 전 발표된 그룹사 실적 
발표와 상반된 모습이었다. 시장이 이번 실적에서 확인한 우려는 아래와 같다. 첫째, 
성장과 이익의 괴리다. 관세 영향을 제외한 26.3Q 영업이익은 2.52조원으로 전년 
동기(2.70조원) 대비 오히려 1,770억원 감소했다. 판매 증가에 따른 물량 효과 
(+4,420억원)와 환율 효과 (+5,290억원)가 발생했음에도, 인센티브와 믹스 악화로 이를 
대부분 반납한 것이다. 기아의 외형 성장 상당 부분이 유럽 EV 판매 증가에서 나오는데, 
해당 시장에서는 중국 EV 업체와의 경쟁으로 공격적인 프로모션이 불가피하다. 26.3Q 
유럽 권역에서의 중소형 EV 판매가 사업 계획을 크게 웃돌며 EV 믹스는 13.1%에서 
14.8%로 확대됐다. 시장은 이를 M/S를 위해 수익성을 훼손하고 있다고 받아들이는 
것이다. 둘째, 당장 26.4Q에도 유의미한 수익성 개선이 없을 것이라는 점이다. 기아는 
26.3Q 컨퍼런스 콜에서 4Q 영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이며, 연초 
제시한 연간 가이던스 (영업이익 10.2조원, +8.3% OPM)에 다소 미달할 것이라고 
밝혔다. 중동 분쟁의 영향이 최소 연말까지 이어지고, 원자재와 물류비 부담 또한 
지속될 것이 회사의 판단이다. 무엇보다 원/달러 환율이 26.2Q 평균 약 1,505원에서 
26.3Q 약 1,430원, 현재 1,320~1,340원 수준까지 하락하며 이에 따른 - 영향이 커질 
수 있다. 셋째, 그룹사와의 이익 체력 비교다. 현대차는 26.3Q 파업과 생산 차질로 도매 
판매가 11.3% 감소했음에도 원가 절감을 통해 영업이익이 5.5% 증가했고, 시장 
기대를 상회했다. 반면 기아는 판매가 7.9% 증가했음에도 영업이익이 컨센서스를 20% 
이상 하회했다. 26.3Q 양사 영업이익 격차는 약 8,600억원까지 벌어진 상황이다.  
단기 노이즈를 제외한다면 기아의 기업 가치는 여전히 높다  
그럼에도 당사는 기아의 EV 전략, 낮은 수익성을 이유로 중장기 기업 가치에 대한 
기대를 낮출 이유는 없다고 판단한다. 첫째, 외부 요인을 걷어내면 본원적인 수익 
창출력은 유지되고 있다. 기아는 26.3Q 영업이익률을 낮춘 외부 요인을 원자재 1.2%p, 
중동 판매 차질 0.7%p, 환율 1.7%p 등 총 3.6%p로 분석했다. 즉 중동 분쟁이 
해소되면 상당 부분이 사라질 일회성 요인이다. 둘째, EV 수익성 개선 경로는 HEV가 
걸어온 길과 같다. 기아는 HEV 역시 도입 초기에는 수익을 내지 못했으나 현재는 ICE에 
버금가는 수익성을 내고 있다며, 26~27년 현재의 EV 전략을 유지하겠다고 밝혔다. 
유럽 ICE 시장은 26.3Q 전년 동기 대비 약 20% 축소된 반면 EV 시장은 50% 이상 
성장하고 있다. 전환기에 스케일을 확보하지 못하면 EV 마진을 개선할 방법도 없다. 
기아는 향후 3년간 배터리, 모터, 히트펌프 등 주요 부품에서 연 10% 수준의 원가 
절감을 추진하고, 2~3년 내 EV를 HEV와 같은 수익성 경로에 올려놓겠다는 계획이다. 
셋째, 중장기 성장 계획을 수정하지 않았다. 기아는 27년 사업 계획이 CID 당시 제시한 
숫자와 큰 차이가 없을 것이며, 중장기 영업이익률 10% 목표도 유지했다. IAA 시행에 
대비해 슬로바키아 공장에서 EV를 전량 생산하고 ICE 국내 수출 방안도 검토 중이다. 
주주환원 측면에서도 TSR 35%를 지키고, 자사주 매입보다 배당을 우선하겠다는 
입장이다. 10/30 종가와 최소 DPS 기준만으로도 배당수익률은 6.3%에 달하고 있다.

기아 판매관리비 (좌) 판매관리비율 (우)
  26.4Q 기아 매출액 31.8조원, 영업이익 2.2조원 전망 
기아 26.4Q 실적을 매출액 31.8조원 (+13.2% YoY), 영업이익 2.2조원 (+20.8% YoY, 
+7.0% OPM)으로 전망한다. 26.4Q 판매 성장세는 이어지고, 3Q에 집중 반영된 원자재 
부담 일부 완화와 계절적 성수기 효과로 3Q 대비 수익성은 소폭 개선될 것이다. 다만 
1) 원화 강세에 따른 평균 환율 하락, 2) 유럽 EV 인센티브의 높은 수준 유지 (미국은 
할부 금리 상승으로 QoQ 약 200달러 증가, 유럽은 전분기와 유사), 3) 중동 판매 
차질과 원자재, 물류비 부담 지속을 감안하면 의미 있는 반등은 어렵다. 이는 4Q 
영업이익률이 3Q 수준과 크게 다르지 않을 것이라는 회사의 언급과도 궤를 같이한다. 
기아 목표주가 150,000원으로 하향  
기아 목표주가를 26.4Q 추정치 하향을 반영해 150,000원으로 하향한다. 기아의 판매 
경쟁력은 금번 분기에도 증명됐다. 다만 EV 판매 확대가 수익성 개선으로 연결된다는 
점이 숫자로 확인되기 전까지, 시장은 기아의 성장에 할인율을 적용할 것이다. 중동 
분쟁 해소와 EV 원가 절감이 맞물리는 27년이 기아가 수익성을 증명할 시점이 될 
것이며, 6%를 상회하는 배당수익률이 그때까지 주가의 하방을 지지할 것으로 판단된다.

그림11. 26.2H 기준 중국 국가별 승용차 수출 대수

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상

3Q26Re: 신규 시설 매출 인식 개시 
연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 6,401억원
(+1.0%, OPM 42.9%)로 컨센서스를 상회하는 실적 기록. 1~4공장이 완전 가동을 유지하는 
한편, 5공장은 PPQ 배치 생산 본격화되면서 매출 기여 확대. 상업 생산 전환이 예상되는 
2H27부터 이익 기여 커질 것으로 기대. 
연초 인수한 Rockville 공장은 3Q에도 안정적인 상업 생산을 이어갔으며 2Q 생산 물량의 
매출이 인식되며 매출 성장에 기여. 동사는 Rockville 공장 매출 인식 등을 반영하여 기존 
+15~20% 성장에서 +20%로 연간 가이던스 상향.   
고객사 수주 회복 기대 
3Q26 기준 누적 수주액은 219억 달러로 전분기 대비 소폭 증가했으나 과거 연간 수주액 
증가 대비 부족한 수준. 저조한 수주 실적은 지난해 초 의약품에 대한 관세 불확실성 증가로 
고객사들의 수주 문의가 저조했던 영향. 26년 초부터 관세 불확실성이 낮아지며 수주 문의
는 회복 추세. 약 1년 정도 논의 기간이 소요되는 것을 고려할 때 26년말~27년 초 수주 회
복 확인 가능할 것으로 기대. 
안정적인 공급망 유지가 중요한 CDMO 산업 특성상 노조 파업 가능성도 고객사 수주를 늦
추는 요인으로 판단. 다만, 10/16 인천 고등법원이 쟁의행위 가처분 항고심에 대해 인용 결
정을 하며 기존 3개 공정에서 9개 공정으로 확대되며 파업 우려는 다소 완화. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원 유지 
매수 의견과 목표 주가 200만원을 유지. 원달러 약세에도 불구하고 5공장 Ramp-up과 
Rockville 공장 매출 기여로 견조한 실적 성장 지속될 것으로 예상. 파업 이슈 해소와 대형 
수주 계약이 주가 상승을 이끌 것으로 판단.

STRONG RESILIENCE TO EXTERNAL SHOCKS
, 
SOLID 
MACROECONOMIC FUNDAMENTALS 
◼ 
 Over the past 
 two years
 , India has demonstrated remarkable resilience in the face of external shocks, while its 
macroeconomic fundamentals have remained solid.  
◼ 
 In FY2025/2026  
(ended in March 2026)
 , India was hit by a sharp rise in US tariffs. However, thanks to supportive fiscal 
and monetary policies, 
 real GDP  
grew by 7.8% in FY2026, placing India among the fastest
 -
 growing Asian economies.  
◼ 
 For the current fiscal year, the Indian economy is facing rising energy prices 
 (
due to 
 the conflict in the Middle East and 
increased food prices 
 because 
 of 
an 
unfavourable monsoon
 ) 
as well as 
 monetary tightening. Nevertheless, despite these 
new challenges, real GDP growth is expected to exceed 7%.  
◼ 
 India is currently in a stronger position than it was in 2022 to cope with the new energy shock. The fiscal leeway to support  
the economy has increased with the decline in the fiscal deficit, and inflation is more contained.  
◼ 
 Although 
 rising  
energy prices have put pressure on external accounts, FX reserves 
 remain  
sufficient to cover the country’s 
short
 -
 term external financing needs. 
 Yet, 
downward pressures on the rupee have been 
 significant 
 and 
could persist in the 
short term
 . 
In 
H1 
2026, India’s external accounts withstood the rise in energy prices fairly well
 , notably  
thanks to the sharp increase 
in workers’ remittances. 
 T
he 
widening of the trade deficit was primarily due to the substantial increase in machinery and 
equipment imports 
 while t
 he oil trade deficit increased 
 only 
moderately, as the rise in energy prices was offset by a decline 
in import volumes. 
 However, 
in H2 2026, the oil trade deficit is expected to widen significantly.  
Additionally, large portfolio outflows have exacerbated downward pressure on the rupee, necessitating interventions by 
the central bank in foreign exchange markets and the adoption of regulatory measures to support the currency. Although 
these measures hav
 e led to a rebound in the rupee (at least temporarily), inflationary pressures, volatile portfolio 
investments, and low net foreign direct investments are 
 expected to continue 
 to put pressure on the currency.  
◼ 
 D
ownward pressures on the rupee 
 and 
 rising bond yields will reduce the government's 
 already limited 
 fiscal capacity to 
increase public investment and 
 support economic development. However, this will not undermine its ability to refinance 
its debt, given its safe structure.  
◼ 
 T
he banking sector is sufficiently robust to cope with the expected rise in credit risks.

STRONG ECONOMIC GROWTH DESPITE 
STRUCTURAL CONSTRAINTS 
◼ 
 In October 2026, the IMF revised upward 
 India’s  
medium
 -
 term growth 
 forecast 
 from 6.5% to 6.8%  
per year
 . Nonetheless, 
to achieve such strong growth performance, 
 India will have to adapt to the challenges posed by artificial intelligence  
(AI)
, 
while its infrastructure and education system are not prepared for it.  
◼ 
 Moreover, India’s development remains highly uneven. 
 The middle class is still modest
 . This is explained by a large 
informal economy, heavy concentration of employment in low value
 -
 added sectors such as agriculture, low participation 
rates among women and youth, and limited industrial development. All this, in turn, reflects India’s weak i
 ntegration into 
global value chains.  
◼ 
 India faces two key structural challenges: i
 ) adapting the services sector to AI and ii) accelerating industrial development. 
Even with progress in sectors such as smartphones and pharmaceuticals, India’s industrial base remains too small to 
absorb its vast labour force. 
 Labo
u
r market 
 rigidities  
and the mismatch between training and business needs are holding 
back both the creation of quality jobs and industrial development.  
◼ 
 Low net foreign direct investment is a 
 major  
 obstacle. While 
 India attracts 
 new 
 FDI, retention is weak
 : 
 despite 
improvements in logistics, governance issues and regulatory barriers have 
 led 
 disinvestments to triple over the past five 
years.  
◼ 
 India 
 may fail in completing its 
 transition to a higher
 -
 value, more industrialised and inclusive economy if reforms do not 
accelerate 
 significantly
 . Structural change
 s 
could lag behind pressures from its rapidly growing population and 
 fast 
global 
technological advances
 . 
02 November 2026

### Page 3

EcoInsight

economic
 -
 research.bnpparibas.com

3

INDIA
’S COMPLEX POSITION 
IN INTERNATIONAL TRADE 
Low 
attractiveness  
due to unfavourable business environment and limited integration in global value chains  
◼ 
 Despite very strong growth, India's attractiveness is low relative to the size of its economy. The stock of foreign direct 
investment stood at only 15% of GDP in Q2 2026 compared to 52% of GDP in Vietnam and around 67% of GDP in Thailand 
and Malaysia.  
◼ 
 This lack of appeal can be attributed to 
 India’s still unfavo
 u
rable 
 business environment and 
 its 
 limited integration into 
global value chains. India’s global market share remain
 s 
 low (1.9% of total 
 world 
 exports  
 of goods
 )
, 
 especially in 
comparison with that of China (16.1% of total exports).  
◼ 
 India has not benefited from the AI boom
 , 
since its exports of AI
 -
 related goods remain modest (0.5% of its total exports)
 . 
However, 
 it has benefited from the “China +1” strategy adopted by 
 international 
 companies seeking to diversify their 
production sources. Smartphone exports by India have increased 6.3
 -
 fold over the past five years and accounted for almost 
6% of global exports in 2025 compared to less than 1% five year
 s 
earlier.  
To 
increase its a
 ttractiveness and develop its industrial sector, India has 
 multiplied F
 ree 
T
rade 
 A
greements  
◼ 
 Over the past 18 months, 
 India  
has signed a growing number of free trade agreements 
 – 
with the European Free Trade 
Association in October 2025, the United Kingdom in July 2025, Oman in December 2025, and the European Union in January 
2026
. This 
 should help to develop the industrial base and demonstrates the Modi government's commitment to 
accelerating India's integration into global trade.  
The sectors most likely to benefit from the recent trade agreement with the EU are electronics and semiconductors, 
pharmaceuticals and chemical
 s
, textile and renewable energy technologies. But expanding trade agreements without deep 
domestic improvements in the business environment, governance, education, and infrastructure will be ineffective. India 
will continue to struggle with investment reten
 tion. 
◼ 
 The US
 -
 India bilateral trade agreement has not yet been concluded. The two sides reached a framework for an interim 
Agreement in February 2026, under which the US would apply an 18% reciprocal tariff on Indian goods, with further 
reductions subject to the 
 successful conclusion of the agreement. As of early October, negotiations 
 are 
stalled. Key sticking 
points include market access, particularly 
 in 
India’s sensitive agricultural sector.

COUNTRY RISK PROFILE

Non transfer risk   
India’s external accounts are solid with a low external debt, moderate current account deficit and large FX 
reserves. 
 India’s net debtor position reached only 5.9% of GDP in Q2 2026. In the short term, external accounts 
are expected to deteriorate 
 given 
the rise in energy prices while downward pressures on the rupee should remain 
high.  
◼ 
 External accounts have remained solid 
 despite recent adverse shocks
 . 
 In 2025, external accounts remained solid 
despite the significant increase in US tariffs. In 
 H1 
 2026, they held up well against rising energy prices. Although the 
current account deficit is expected to widen for the current fiscal year as a whole, it should remain contained, and foreign 
exchange reserves should be more than sufficient to cover the co
 untry’s short
 -
 term financing needs. The 
 major  
causes 
for concern are the 
 structurally 
 low level of foreign direct investment (although it is rising) and the increased reliance on 
portfolio investment, which exposes the currency to greater volatility.  
◼ 
 Structural vulnerability to energy price shock
 . 
India is structurally vulnerable to an energy price shock as its 
 oil and gas  
trade deficit is relatively high (2.8% of GDP in 2025). However, in H1 2026, the current account withstood fairly well the 
rise in energy prices especially thanks to the sharp increase in workers’ remittances  
(+0.7 pp to 3.7% of GDP)
 . The trade 
deficit widened significantly but principally because of the strong increase in machinery and equipment imports rather

Systemic credit risk  
The banking sector has strengthened over the past five years
 , 
as illustrated by the improvement in 
 asset  
quality 
and capital buffers. The main sources of credit risks are rising household debt, the rise in energy prices, the 
unfavourable monsoon and monetary tightening.  
◼ 
 The quality of assets has improved significantly. 
 In Q1 2026, the banking sector’s non
 -
 performing loan (NPL) ratio fell 
to a multi
 -
 decade low of 1.8%. For public banks  
(
54.4% of total loans
 )
, the NPL ratio declined to 2.1%.  
◼ 
 Capital buffers are strong. 
 While 
loan loss provisions remained modest at 75.6% of NPLs, capital buffers stayed strong. 
The Capital Adequacy Ratio (CAR) rose to 17.7% in Q1 2026, up from 17.4% a year earlier. From April 2027, Indian banks 
will adopt a new Expected Credit Loss (ECL) framewo
 rk, requiring earlier provisioning for potential loan losses. In the 
short term, higher provisioning requirements could push up lending rates and tighten credit conditions 
 while 
 triggering 
capital raising or targeted government recapitalisation. 
 I
n the medium term, the reform could strengthen banking  
sector 
resilience through earlier recognition of credit risks and more robust loss buffers.  
◼ 
 Banks’

 is

 .

deposits.

 Profitability remains satisfactory. 
 It

margins, but remains satisfactory. In Q2 2026, 
 the

13.0%, respectively (IMF data).

 P
rivate

 ,

Non
-

debt was 56% of GDP in Q1 2026. Listed firms’ financial positions continued to strengthen amid 
surging profits, improving their ability to service debt. In Q2 2026, the 
 average

from 6.1 a year earlier. However, the share of companies with an ICR below 1 (insufficient earnings to cover interest costs) 
increased to 18.5%. Stress remain

Household leverage requires monitoring. Although still moderate, household debt increased to 49.1% of GDP in Q1 2026, 
up from 42.7% five years earlier, driven largely by non

◼ 
 Monetary 
 policy

 Due to rising  
inflation (+5.5% year
 -

-

rupee, the RBI raised its policy rate by 25 basis points to 5.5% in October 2026 under a "calibrated tightening" stance.  
◼ 
 T
he banking sector should remain resilient

 Due to rising energy prices, a poor

and small enterprises.

arly warning signs are emerging

The bank
for a changing
world
DO ITALIAN HOUSEHOLDS HAVE WHAT IT TAKES TO FINANCE EUROPE’S NEXT IDEAS?
Andrea d’Aquino
Between 2019 and 2025, US value added grew at twice the pace of Europe’s, driven largely by technology and labour productivity 1,2. Yet Europe
does not lack saving to finance a similar development: over the past ten years, Euro area gross national saving exceeded domestic investment
by about €300 bn a year, equivalent to 15% of productive investment 3, and in 2024-2025 gross capital outflows to the United States averaged

€785 billion a year 4. The situation described therefore creates a “European paradox”, where household particularly save but do not take on so
much risk to foster innovation as in the US.
Safety versus risk: how Italian and European households differ
In Q2 2026, Euro area households’ gross financial wealth grew by 6.5% y/y to approximately €37 trillion, of which just over 30% is held in
currency and deposits (€11,165 billion), compared with about 10% in the United States. Italy has the lowest share of currency and deposits

among the major Euro area economies (see Chart 1). In Q2 2026, Italian households’ financial wealth grew by 6.3% y/y, in line with the
Euro area, reaching €6,609 billion, of which 24.2% is held in currency and deposits (about €1,598 billion), compared with 28.3% in France
(€2,151 billion), 32% in Spain (€1,168 billion) and 36% in Germany (€3,660 billion).
1 Fabio Panetta; Il risparmio nella società che cambia, Banca d’Italia; 30th October 2026.
2 Luisa Carpinelli et al.; The Great Divide: productivity dynamics in the United States and euro area after the pandemic; Questioni di Economia e Finanza n. 924, Banca d’Italia; April

2025.
3 Productive investments are gross fixed investments minus residential construction.
4 It includes the item ‘other investments’, mainly made up of deposits, loans, and trade receivables.
SOURCE: EUROSTAT, BNL ECONOMIC RESEARCH
In Italy the largest share of households’ total financial assets is invested in equity
and investment fund shares, less in currency and deposits

Italian risk is tied up mostly in unlisted firms
Judging by this metric, European households’ risk appetite is lower than that of their US counterparts. Another metric to look at: the listed
shares. They account for 5% of Euro area gross financial wealth 5 (3.1% in Italy, 3.7% in France, 6.6% in Spain, 7.2% in Germany). However,
once holdings in unlisted companies are included, the share of risk capital rises to 15% in the Euro area, 34% in the United States and

more than 20% in Italy.
A large part of the gap reflects the wider presence in Europe of unlisted firms, largely family-owned. In Italy in particular, unlisted shares
account for 17.1% of gross financial wealth, just over 7 pp above the Euro area average, compared with 12.4% in France, 7.7% in Spain and
2.6% in Germany (see Chart 2). Unlisted family firms are on average smaller and less productive than the system as a whole, especially
among medium-sized and large firms and in technology- and knowledge-intensive sectors 6.

Italian households’ risk capital is therefore largely held in forms that are hard to diversify and in firms far from the technological frontier,
while the risk capital reaching the youngest firms (less than 12 years old) is scarce, even though these firms are very active in innovation:
innovative start-ups account for just over 2% of young firms but hold about 44% of that category’s patents. Similarly, venture-capital-
backed firms are barely 0.2% of young firms but account for around 12% of total patents of the youngest firms 7.

Italian inheritances will largely pass on bricks
Investment choices are also linked to demographics: risk appetite tends to rise until adulthood and then declines with advancing age 8.
According to Banca d’Italia 9, in the coming years in Italy, those born between 1966 and 1975 will benefit from wealth transfers to a greater
extent than earlier cohorts: inheritances would account for around 40% of their wealth, compared with 30% for those born between 1951

and 1965. All else being equal, households expecting to receive an inheritance hold more financial assets. What will change hands is
mainly real assets, above all housing: in 2022 (latest available data), households whose reference person was born between 1941 and
1950 held on average around 62% of their total wealth in dwellings, 28% in financial assets and almost 10% in equity stakes in businesses.
As a result, once households receive the inheritance, their portfolios could become more tilted towards housing, which may lead to a

rebalancing towards financial assets.
The challenge for the Euro area in the coming years will be to press ahead with the creation of a single market for capital, in order to
provide greater investment opportunities and avert the “European savings paradox”.
andrea.aquino@bnpparibas.com

7 Maria Giulia Cassinis et al.; Innovative firms unveiled: economic and financial insight from Italian start-ups; Questioni di Economia e Finanza n. 967, Banca d’Italia; October 2025.

Document 336: Fed's Hawks Are Squawking

Source PDF: all report pdf/Fed's Hawks Are Squawking.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-19

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Fed's Hawks Are Squawking
Date:    9 hours ago
Fed's Hawks Are Squawking

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Fed's Hawks Are Squawking
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 18 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Jiri Plistil / Unsplash

I. Markets In An AI Fog
Stocks were mixed in a lackluster session today as investors navigated
the fog around the AI trade and the Fed's next move. The S&P 500
slipped 0.2%, the DJIA fell 1.1%, the Nasdaq gained 0.4%, and the
Russell 2000 rose 0.5% (chart).

### Page 2

Nvidia's shares declined 2.0% today despite reporting impressive Q3
earnings growth and upbeat Q4 and FY 2027 guidance (charts). The stock
seems cheap at a forward multiple of 11.0. The S&P 500 Semiconductor
industry trades at nearly the identical forward P/E, of 11.1, representing a
significant discount to the S&P 500's valuation multiple of 17.6.
Investors are focusing on Nvidia's more rigorous and disciplined approach
to financing the AI boom and uncertainty over who will ultimately succeed

Jensen Huang. They also still want to know how much of Q3's revenues
were attributable to China's purchases of banned Nvidia chips. Anthropic's
Thursday IPO will provide the next key catalyst for the AI trade.

### Page 3

Bearish oil fundamentals continue to wrestle with the geopolitical risk
premium embedded in crude prices. The Energy Information
Administration’s report today showed US crude inventories rising 3.4
million barrels in the week ended November 13. However, the Middle East
conflict isn't over and could still re-escalate. Fundamentals won the
market’s tug-of-war today, with the price of West Texas Intermediate crude
falling 3.2% to $83.26 a barrel. As a result, the 10-year Treasury yield

eased 4bps to 5.07% (chart).
II. The Fed's Warsh Problem
At its October meeting, the FOMC voted 10-2 to hold the federal funds
rate (FFR) steady rather than hike it by 25bps. The October FOMC
meeting minutes, released today, indicated that policymakers wanted

### Page 4

more time to assess the AI investment cycle, the effects of September's
rate hike, and geopolitical developments. Most FOMC participants
acknowledged that inflation remained troublesome, while some warned
that further delays could necessitate more disruptive tightening later.
Since October, economic growth has remained strong, the labor market
has improved, and inflation pressures have mounted. The incoming data
increasingly suggest that the Fed should have hiked in October. The FFR

futures market now anticipates two 25bps rate hikes over the next six
months (chart).
As a result, Fed officials are increasingly calling for greater urgency in
tightening monetary policy. Dallas Fed President Lorie Logan warned that
rates may need to rise faster, while St. Louis Fed President Alberto
Musalem sees several hikes before spring. Governor Christopher Waller
argued Wednesday that the Fed must reverse all three of last year's
25bps cuts and “may need to do more.”

Waller outlined two options: (1) hike 50bps in December or (2) hike 25bps
while signaling another increase in January. But Chairman Kevin Warsh is
averse to forward guidance, which nixes the second option and may leave
the Fed with no choice but to proceed with a larger hike to demonstrate
urgency.
Importantly, the October meeting’s minutes revealed that some officials
favoring a hike would have accepted a hold if the Fed had clearly

### Page 5

communicated further tightening. Warsh's communication philosophy may
thus be limiting the Fed's flexibility precisely when it needs it most.
We continue to expect a 50bps rate hike at the December meeting.
III. US Economy Still Roaring
This week's economic releases offered further evidence of an economy
that is firing on all cylinders:
(1) Retail sales. October retail sales rose 0.2% m/m, weighed down by a
1.6% decline in auto sales and a 2.3% drop in furniture sales (chart).

Underlying spending was considerably stronger, with sales excluding
autos rising 0.6% m/m. Eight of the 13 major retail categories posted
gains, led by miscellaneous store retailers (+4.7%), gasoline stations
(+1.5%), health and personal care stores (+1.5%), and nonstore retailers
(+1.2%).
Control-group retail sales, used in calculating GDP, rose a solid 0.6% m/m
in October, following an upwardly revised 0.5% gain in September (chart).

### Page 6

The latest Redbook report reinforces that positive assessment. Same-
store sales rose 9.8% y/y in the week ended November 13 (chart). With
Redbook sales growth holding near double-digit rates, there is little
evidence of a slowdown in consumer spending heading into the holiday
season.
(2) Industrial production. Tuesday's October industrial production report
showed factory activity continues to improve for the second consecutive
year after a prolonged soft patch from 2022 through 2024 (chart).

Manufacturing output rose 0.3% m/m for the second consecutive month,
even as overall industrial production fell 0.4%, largely reflecting a sharp
4.3% decline in utilities output.

### Page 7

The details suggest that the AI buildout remains a key driver of industrial
growth (chart).
Since ChatGPT's release in late 2022, technology-related industrial
production has been soaring to record highs (chart).

### Page 8

Electric and gas utilities are increasing their output to meet the needs of
data centers (chart).
Defense spending is providing another tailwind for industrial activity
(chart).

### Page 9

(3) Employment. The ADP employment report for the four weeks through
October 31 showed a solid increase of 23,750 (chart).
💡

featured in our segment, "Ed Answers Your Questions".

Document 340: FX Daily_ Carry trade unwind in full swing

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Carry trade unwind in full swing.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

The prospect of faster Fed tightening and continued dislocation
in tech stocks is sucking funds into the dollar and the yen.
Popular FX carry trade positions are being unwound across the
board.  Given that a hawkish Fed lies at the heart of the
current correction in risk assets, and there are no signs of
Fed rhetoric softening anytime soon, USD can stay strong

Chris Turner, Francesco Pesole and Frantisek Taborsky
The prospect of faster Fed tightening is starting to weigh heavily on risk assets
and helping the dollar
USD: The Waller correction
After another day of losses, the US S&P 500 index is now 8.5% oﬀ
its highs of the year. The extent of this correction is not far from that
seen in March this year (9.5%) and way oﬀ the 'Liberation Day' rout
of 21% seen in March/April 2025. The cause of the current correction
has been the threat of a faster Federal Reserve tightening cycle, put
on the table by the Fed's Christopher Waller last week. We
discussed high US real interest rates here yesterday. And the
volatility in tech stocks may also be owing to this week's Anthropic

### Page 2

IPO, where investors have probably been shuﬄing portfolios to
participate in a $100bn IPO. The fact that the Fed wants to tighten
faster because of a demand shock should not necessarily be bad
news for equities and, barring some major earning misses, the case
for another 10% correction in equities is a weak one.
Yet there seems no reason for the Fed to drop its hawkish rhetoric
anytime soon. Next Wednesday sees the release of the Fed's
preferred inﬂation measure – the core PCE index. The October data

is expected to show the year-on-year rate rising to 3.2% with
upside risks, and conﬁrm that the Fed will need to take action.
For FX, that means a continued strong dollar and continued
pressure on popular carry trades. The Latam complex has had a
terrible week, with the most popular carry position, the Colombian
peso, oﬀ heavily. Brazil yesterday stepped in with FX intervention,
but $1bn in sales was quickly overwhelmed. Elsewhere, the big drop
in metals has left the South African rand vulnerable, and a dovish

central bank has seen an unwind of crowded positions in Hungary's
forint. We are also starting to see expectations build for rate cuts in
Australia next year on the view that the Reserve Bank of Australia
has overtightened. We highlighted this as one of the key risks in our
2027 FX Outlook. Pressure may also be building on long positions in
the Turkish lira, where implied yields are starting to rise again.
A big carry trade unwind and emerging markets sell-oﬀ remain a

positive for the dollar, and to a lesser degree, the yen. We expect the
dollar to hold gains into next week and keep 104.60 as a near-term
DXY target.
The US data calendar is light today, and the only Fed speakers are
the hawkish Waller (10:15CET) and Lisa Cook, who surprisingly
voted for a hike at the October FOMC meeting.
Chris Turner

traders suggest the low volatility price action here is a function of a

Chris Turner
GBP: New EU-UK agreements announced
At a press conference in Brussels today, UK Prime Minister Andy
Burnham and EU leaders are expected to announce three new
agreements. The ﬁrst is on SPS (Sanitary and Phytosanitary
measures), which will reduce border checks on trade in food,

animals and plants. The second is on a greater alignment between
the separate EU and UK Emissions Trading Schemes (ETS), where
greater recognition of each other's schemes can reduce carbon-
related friction in trade – especially important for the steel and
manufacturing sectors. The third looks at the thorny issue of
mobility in the Youth Experience Scheme (YES), where up to
100,000 18-31 year-olds can obtain work visas for up to three
years. Burnham will be marketing these deals as addressing the

cost-of-living crisis and giving young Britons valuable opportunities
overseas.

The deals, as always, will be subject to last-minute negotiation and
serve as a reminder to the Burnham government that 'cherry-

That may sway the Labour Party against any commitment to
rejoin the EU in its next election manifesto – which could potentially
emerge as early as next year.
Sterling typically responds well to the prospect of closer UK ties with
Europe on the view that reversing a bad Brexit deal can generate a

1-2% larger UK economy in 5-10 years' time. Today's agreements
are fully expected, however, and may not generate any
independent sterling strength.
That said, we like sterling over the coming months on the view that
Bank of England tightening expectations prove more resilient than
those of the ECB. This corrective EUR/GBP rally may again stall in
the 0.8400 area, and we are happy with a year-end forecast of
0.8300.
Chris Turner

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
Neuralink 近⽇发布视频，展⽰⼀名失去双臂功能的瘫痪患者凭借植⼊的 N1 脑机接⼝芯⽚，
仅⽤意念便在新作《 GTA 6 》中完成驾驶快艇、⽔上⻜机及格⽃等复杂操作，彻底告别此
前 “ 嘴含棍⼦戳 iPad” 的困境。
摘要：
⻢斯克旗下脑机接⼝公司 Neuralink 再度展⽰其技术突破 —— ⼀名失去双臂功能的瘫痪患者，借
助植⼊式芯⽚，仅凭意念便在热⻔新作《 GTA 6 》中驾驶快艇、驾驶⽔上⻜机并完成徒⼿格⽃。
Neuralink 近⽇在其 X 账号发布相关视频，记录了上述患者全程⽤脑机接⼝（ BCI ）操控游戏的过
程。这名患者表⽰，⼿术前只能靠 " 嘴含棍⼦戳 iPad" 来玩游戏，⽽植⼊芯⽚后的感受是 " 你⽴刻
重新拥有了通往世界的⼊⼝ " 。
《 GTA 6 》本周正式发售，预售销量已达 3250 万份。 Neuralink 选择在此节点发布视频，进⼀步

凸显其技术的实⽤价值，也为这家初创公司在公众视野中积累了新的曝光。
从 " 啄⻝ iPad" 到意念操控
据 Neuralink 发布的视频，这名患者在植⼊ N1 芯⽚后，得以通过⼤脑信号直接操作游戏，完成了
快艇驾驶、⽔上⻜机⻜⾏及⾁搏战等复杂操作。他描述，使⽤嘴棍操控 iPad 的旧⽅式令他 " 像⼀
只⻦⼀样不停地啄 " ，⽽脑机接⼝的引⼊从根本上改变了这⼀体验。
这并⾮ Neuralink ⾸次展⽰其在游戏⽆障碍领域的能⼒。 2024 年 3 ⽉， Neuralink 曾公开第⼀位接
受植⼊⼿术的⼈类患者 Noland Arbaugh 通过芯⽚操控国际象棋、《⽂明 VI 》及《⻢⾥奥卡丁⻋》
的画⾯。据报道，尽管此后部分连接电极出现脱落， Arbaugh 对游戏的控制能⼒仍持续提升。
需求远超供给，技术普及仍⾯临制约
游戏控制之外， Neuralink 今年已取得多项重要进展。今年 3 ⽉，该公司宣布成功帮助⼀名患有渐
冻症（ ALS ）的患者 Kenneth Shock ，将其脑电信号实时转化为可被听到的语⾳ —— 这是

Neuralink 在语⾔辅助领域迈出的关键⼀步。 ALS 可导致严重的⾔语障碍，⽽该技术为此类患者
提供了全新的沟通途径。
尽管技术层⾯持续取得突破， Neuralink ⽬前的覆盖能⼒仍⼗分有限。据报道，截⾄ 2025 年底，
已有约 1 万名有意愿接受 N1 芯⽚植⼊的患者排队等候。按照公司现⾏标准，申请者须因颈椎脊髓
损伤或 ALS 导致⼿部功能严重受损乃⾄丧失，⽅可获得资格。
这意味着，对于数以万计仍在等待的残障⽤⼾⽽⾔，脑机接⼝技术在短期内尚⽆法形成规模化
解 决 ⽅ 案 。 技 术 突 破 的 ⽰ 范 效 应 固 然 ⿎ 舞 ⼈ ⼼ ， 但 从 实 验 室 成 果 到 ⼴ 泛 临 床 应 ⽤ 之 间 ，
Neuralink 仍⾯临较⻓的路要⾛。

Standard Chartered Bank

Charts of the week: Watching earnings and a potential ‘Blue wave’

US Big Tech earnings, mid-term elections and major central bank policy meetings are the near-term market drivers 
Consensus S&P500 index Q3 earnings estimates by sector   Chance of Democrats winning control of US House and Senate*

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *Polymarket probability

Editorial 
Policy, politics and earnings  
Strategy summary: We retain our near-term defensive and 
diversified stance across assets, sectors and regions amid 
near-term uncertainty around monetary policy, politics and 
earnings. The next two weeks will be busy for major central 
banks, US political watchers and technology leaders powering 
the AI supercycle. We expect the Fed, ECB and BoJ to hold 
rates next week, but markets will focus on whether central 
banks are turning more hawkish due to the latest surge in fuel 
prices. The US mid-term elections are expected to give 
Democrats control of the House and Senate, with implications 
for taxes and the AI-led investment boom.  
Meanwhile, the consensus is expecting the US tech sector to 
report over 60% y/y earnings growth for Q3, the biggest jump 
in over two decades outside the post-COVID rebound.  Each of 
these three drivers have the potential to surprise markets.  
Big Three to hold monetary policy: We expect the Fed, ECB 
and BoJ to hold policy rates next week, after September’s 
hikes, as they assess whether higher bond yields have 
tightened financial conditions enough to offset fuel-price-driven 
inflation. We expect the Big Three to hike next in December as 
fuel prices are likely to stay elevated into early next year due to 
refining capacity constraints. Nevertheless, the Fed and ECB 
are likely to deliver less than around 60bps of rate hikes priced 
in by money markets over the next 12 months on the back of 
an eventual easing of oil and fuel prices leading to disinflation 
by H2 2027. This outlook means 3-7-year Developed Market 
investment grade bonds currently offer attractive risk-reward.  
Opportunity in Australian bonds. Other major central banks, 
such as the Bank of England, Bank of Canada and Reserve 
Bank of New Zealand are likely to hike rates by 25bps in the 
next two weeks given their focus on taming near-term inflation. 
However, we believe the Reserve Bank of Australia (RBA) is 
close to the end of its hiking cycle, even if it hikes one last time 
in the coming months (money markets give a 60% chance of a 
hike on 3 November). Australia’s domestic labour and housing 
markets and business confidence are softening after the RBA 
hiked its policy rate four times this year to a 15-year high. 
Meanwhile, Australia’s cash rate is 60bps higher than the US 
policy rate, supporting the AUD. We continue to see attractive 
income and FX gain opportunities from AUD corporate bonds.  
A ‘Blue Wave’ in the US? Prediction markets strongly favour 
Democrats taking the House of Representatives and possibly 
the Senate in the 3 November US mid-term elections. While an 
opposition sweep in the mid-terms has historically delivered the 
best 12-month returns for equities among all scenarios, partly 
due to the resulting policy gridlock, a decisive Democratic “blue 
wave” could increase longer-term risks of higher taxes and 
uncertainty over policy towards AI investment and data centres. 
With the Senate race still close and prediction markets fallible, 
we remain cautious on risk assets in the near term.  
Closing EU bank contingent convertible bonds (CoCos1) 
and US 10-year government bond ideas: We lock in 2% 
gains in EU CoCos and 1.7% gain in US 10-year government 
bond ideas amid French and US political risks (page 5-6). We 
continue to hedge any inflation risks through inflation-protected 
bonds and geopolitical risk through an Overweight in gold.  
Watching Big Tech earnings:  Upcoming Big Tech earnings 
raises uncertainty as market leadership remains concentrated 
and share-price reactions more mixed. With valuations less 
likely to drive returns, earnings delivery and sustainability of AI 
investment, funding and monetisation and broadening out of 
earnings beyond tech will be critical, raising the risk of volatility 
and sector divergence. We remain invested but defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification across sectors and regions.  
Staying defensive and diversified. We recently upgraded the 
defensive US Healthcare sector to Overweight, while retaining 
our Overweights to US tech and materials as well as financials 
in the US, Euro area and Japan. We also downgraded the more 
volatile Communications Services sector to Neutral and took 
profits on the semiconductor segment earlier this month before 
the latest correction. Our defensive and diversified stance 
should preserve participation in further equity upside, while 
reducing exposure to earnings disappointments and supporting 
resilience through the reporting season.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions 
  What are the key takeaways from the technology sector 
earnings reported so far this week? 
Our view: Recent technology sector earnings results point to 
strong compute demand and continued capex expansion, 
supporting our positive long-term view on the AI investment 
theme. However, rising funding and execution risks warrant 
near-term caution. We prefer to avoid increasing exposure to 
higher-risk areas within the technology sector for now. 
Rationale: Earnings results reported this week suggest major tech 
companies remain committed to expanding their AI infrastructure. 
While this bodes well for AI infrastructure providers, ambitious 
expansion plans imply that an increasing share of AI capex is likely 
to depend on debt financing. We expect debt-financed AI capex to 
rise from c.25% of AI capex in 2026 to c.40% in 2027.  
Greater reliance on borrowing increases sensitivity to interest rates, 
while deployment delays could postpone revenue recognition amid 
ongoing financing costs. However, evidence of improving AI 
monetisation and adoption should strengthen operating cash 
flows and support internal capex funding. Against this backdrop, 
we will assess Big Tech earnings next week for greater clarity on 
capex guidance and funding and AI monetisation trends.  
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  Are you still positive on Euro area bank equities amid 
heightened political and fiscal uncertainty in the region? 
Our view: We remain positive on Euro area bank equities, 
supported by resilient earnings and shareholder-friendly capital 
return policies. While France’s fiscal uncertainty and a softer 
growth outlook warrant caution, the recent pullback in Euro 
area bank equities offers selective opportunities. We favour 
well-capitalised banks with strong deposit franchises and 
diversified earnings. 
Rationale: We expect Euro area banks’ Q3 2026 net interest 
income to grow 1.5-3.0% q/q, led by Spanish banks, driven by 
higher rates and continued asset repricing. Resilient fee income from 
wealth management and investment banking activity, as well as 
dividends and buybacks, should provide further support. Price-to-
book (P/B) valuations have eased, despite expectations for 
healthy return on equity (ROE), thus improving risk-reward. 
Fiscal uncertainty in France has widened sovereign spreads, 
pressuring banks’ bond holdings and funding costs. Although 
French banks represent 13% of the Euro area Bank Index, volatility 
could spread to regional financial markets. However, stress in the 
broader banking system appears limited, supported by strong 
capital buffers and ECB tools to contain disorderly market moves and 
sovereign market contagion. We will continue to monitor credit 
trends, sovereign spreads and banks’ 2027 earnings guidance. 
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What has driven the recent weakness in China equities, 
and what is your outlook from here? 
Our view: We maintain China equities as a Core holding, as 
valuations remain inexpensive. Rapid technological innovation 
and investment in AI localisation provide tailwinds, but the 
potential for a re-rating appears delayed. Investors should seek 
to diversify their portfolios and reduce concentration risk. 
Rationale: China equities have weakened, driven by mounting 
scepticism over AI capex returns, structural margin 
compression and concerns about the country’s significant 
financing requirements. Another notable feature in China has been 
significant share issuance, which has diluted earnings per share and 
weighed on the MSCI China Index’s performance. These pressures 
have been compounded by geopolitical headwinds, restrictions on 
advanced chips, an enduring property sector contraction and weak 
domestic demand. September’s stimulus measures also 
disappointed markets in terms of their scale and pace of delivery. 
Notwithstanding these headwinds, valuations remain inexpensive, 
supported by rapid domestic innovation and structural tailwinds 
in AI localisation, leading us to retain China equities as a Core 
holding. Elevated volatility reinforces the need to be defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification rather than relying on technology 
sector concentration. We also remain Overweight China’s 
healthcare, financial and materials sectors. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Are you still bullish on US 10-year government bonds?   
Our view: We are closing our opportunistic bullish idea on US 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. The risk-reward 
has become less compelling after the yield broke below 5%, the 
lower bound of our three-month forecast range of 5.00-5.25%. 
Rationale: The US 10-year yield fell over 17bps to 4.97% on 22 
October – its first move below 5% since 23 September and biggest 
one-day drop since March 2023. A sell-off in AI-related equities and 
renewed Middle East tensions drove investors towards safe havens. 
Earlier this month, the yield hit its highest level in over two decades. 
The forces behind the rally are fragile, though. Oil prices remain 
elevated amid escalating Middle East tensions, and September US 
consumer inflation rose to 3.7% y/y. Fed officials have signalled 
there is no urgency to raise rates at the 27-28 October meeting, 
but persistent price pressures keep further tightening on the table. 
We prefer to lock in gains and close our opportunistic bullish idea on 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO, Fixed Income & FX

Significant share issuance has kept the market 
capitalisation fairly stable, while the MSCI China

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is your outlook for French bank bonds, especially 
ahead of France’s upcoming sovereign credit rating reviews?   
Our view: The recent sell-off in French bank bonds is a repricing 
of tail risks, not a credit story. We expect further price volatility 
until the April 2027 French presidential election, particularly at 
the junior end of the capital structure, so we are closing our 
opportunistic EU bank contingent convertibles (CoCos1) idea at 
a gain of 2.0%. 
Rationale: We believe the recent sell-off in French bank bonds 
reflects domestic political and fiscal risks rather than weaker 
credit fundamentals. The sustained widening of the OAT-Bund 
spread has raised concerns about the profit and loss (P&L) impact 
on banks' sovereign debt holdings. Banks provide limited 
disclosure on these exposures. However, banks’ investment 
portfolios are generally diversified, and holdings booked at amortised 
cost should soften the P&L impact. Near-term catalysts include the 
upcoming sovereign credit rating reviews. A sovereign downgrade 
could trigger bank rating downgrades, with domestically focused 
lenders potentially more exposed than regionally diversified peers. A 
sovereign credit event is not our base case, but we expect headline 
risks to persist until the French presidential election. Against this 
backdrop, we are closing our opportunistic EU bank CoCos idea. 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Will the November US mid-term elections and European 
bond volatility support a sustained Swiss franc (CHF) rebound?  
Our view: We expect a near-term CHF rebound, supported 
mainly by safe-haven demand amid European bond volatility. 
We believe the US mid-term elections will be a secondary driver. 
Rationale: European sovereign bond volatility provides the stronger 
catalyst for CHF strength. The France-Germany government bond 
yield spread remains elevated near 160bps, reflecting heightened 
French fiscal concerns and reinforcing the CHF’s safe-haven appeal. 
The US mid-terms could provide additional support for the CHF. 
A Democratic victory in either chamber is expected to constrain 
further fiscal expansion, potentially weighing on the USD. However, 
this would not necessarily lead to faster fiscal consolidation. 
The SNB’s 0% policy rate leaves the CHF at a sizeable interest-
rate disadvantage vs. the USD, constraining sustained CHF gains. 
With EUR/CHF below 0.90, excessive CHF strength could also 
increase the risk of SNB intervention. A decisive USD/CHF break 
below 0.8130 would strengthen the technical case for a more 
sustained CHF rebound.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

A widening France-Germany 10-year government 
bond yield spread and a stable investment-grade 
(IG) bond option-adjusted spread (OAS) suggest a 
repricing of political and fiscal risk rather than 
credit risk

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Would worsening budget deficits and political uncertainty 
across parts of Europe affect your view on EUR/USD and Euro 
area government bond yields? 
Our view: Fiscal and political concerns constrain near-term 
EUR/USD gains, with France being the primary vulnerability. We 
expect EUR/USD to recover towards the 1.17-1.18 range over 
the next 12 months, supported by continued Euro area 
economic growth and an expected fading of USD support. We 
expect wider differentials in Euro area government bond yields, 
with political risk premium elevated in French government 
bonds (OATs) and German government bonds (Bunds) 
supported by safe-haven demand. 
Rationale: France's high public debt, large budget deficit and 
political obstacles to fiscal adjustments are weighing on market 
confidence, with the country’s 10-year government bond yield 
rising above 5%. The risk of stress spreading to Italy and Spain 
adds to regional concerns, although current evidence does not 
establish a broad European fiscal crisis. German government bonds 
should benefit from safe-haven demand, while fiscal concerns keep 
risk premia on vulnerable countries' bonds elevated. EUR/USD 
remains more sensitive to US-Germany interest-rate differentials 
and broader market confidence than to French bond yields alone. 
France’s fiscal risks, elevated energy costs and high US interest 
rates weigh on the EUR in the near term. Over the next 12 months, 
Germany’s multi-year public investment programme, resilient 
exports and sound banking-sector fundamentals are expected to 
support Euro area growth. We expect the Fed's rate-hiking cycle to 
peak by mid-2027, reducing USD support and driving the EUR  
towards 1.17-1.18 . 
The principal tail risk is fiscal stress widening government bond 
yield spreads and spilling over into bank funding conditions and 
lending activity, weakening growth and delaying the EUR's recovery. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

France’s fiscal risks constrain near-term EUR/USD 
gains, but a modest recovery remains likely

• Taiwan’s exports could moderate as the AI cycle normalizes

### Page 2

The Week Ahead November 6, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         2

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Taiwan

Export growth is expected to ease to 
44.4% yoy in October from 60.9% in 
September. A higher comparison base 
makes exceptional export growth 
difficult to sustain from 4Q onwards. The 
global AI cycle is also expected to settle 
into a more normal pace into 2027, as 
higher interest rates lift capital costs 
and US mid-term election results 
heighten regulatory uncertainty. These 
factors underpin our forecast for 
Taiwan’s GDP growth to return to high 
single digits from 4Q onwards and into 
2027.

Malaysia

We expect Malaysia’s industrial 
production growth to rise to 5.5% yoy in 
September 2026 from 5.0% yoy in 
August. The uptick was likely driven by 
export-oriented manufacturing as 
strong artificial intelligence (AI)-related 
demand continued to support growth in 
electrical and electronics goods, 
alongside a smaller contraction in mining.

China

Export growth is expected to decelerate 
from 36.3% yoy in September to 28.1% in 
October. Both the official manufacturing 
PMI and new export orders sub-index fell 
below 50 after two months in expansion 
territory. High-frequency trade indicators 
also point to slowing momentum, with 
average daily deadweight tonnage handled 
at China’s 20 major ports falling from 7.2% 
yoy in September to -4.2% in October. 
Freight activity weakened as well, with 
growth in international cargo flights 
dropping from 3.6% yoy to -5.9% over the 
same period. On the price front, factory-gate 
and raw material price pressures moderated, 
with the main raw material purchasing price 
and producer price sub-indices declining 
from 60.8 and 54.0 to 56.8 and 51.7, 
respectively. Despite remaining above the 
50 threshold, the moderation suggests some 
easing in pipeline price pressures. 
Downstream consumer price pressures are 
also likely to remain subdued amid soft 
domestic demand. As a result, CPI inflation 
is expected to ease slightly from 0.9% yoy in 
September to 0.8% in October.

India

We expect headline inflation to rise to 6.0% 
yoy in October from 5.5% the month before, 
on higher food costs and a low base effect. 
High frequency data pointed to a further 
pick-up in sequential pressures in food 
segments, across cereals, edible oil, 
perishables (vegetables) etc. Non-food 
components should find relief from further 
moderation in precious metals and delay in 
passage of retail fuel price changes. Core 
inflation is nonetheless set to quicken to 4.5-
4.6% as services notch increases and as 
indirect tax effects in the base, fall out of the 
equation. With inflation expected to test 
past the higher end of the RBI target range, 
we expect a second successive rate hike in 
December. The decline in oil prices has also 
led to cooling of overnight OIS rates as 
concerns over aggressive rate hikes have 
eased. Goods trade deficit is likely to stay 
wide at above $30bn in Oct.

Document 464: 【國泰證期研究部】伺服器產業-28年資料中心擴建不確定性擴增-20261123

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】伺服器產業-28年資料中心擴建不確定性擴增-20261123.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-23

Status: ok

### Page 1

1 
產業更新-伺服器產業 
28 年資料中心擴建不確定性擴增 
What’s New–BIS 管制將使小型 Neocloud 受影響 
BIS 加大對於海外算力管制範圍，將加強審查海外資料中心的最終租戶及母公司不可來自中國，我們認
為此將影響海外小型 Neocloud 訂單，觀察本月阿里巴巴下修 28 財年(2027/4/1～2028/3/31)雲端營
收成長至 30~45%，騰訊部分預付算力合約可能將於後續季度回沖，皆顯示海外算力控管將影響中系
CSP 算力分配規劃。預期主要影響會在小型 Neocloud，其資料中心主要位於東南亞等地，承接中國
客戶訂單比重較高也為主要審查地點。我們觀察美系大型 CSP 八成以上的資料中心用電量位於歐美等
地，且中大型 Neocloud 包含 CoreWeave、Nebius 亦有逾半在手訂單來自美系大型 CSP，相對受
此管制影響較低。短期風險將集中於對中系客戶依賴較高的小型雲端業者。對於大型 CSP 與中大型
Neocloud 影響有限。 
Trends/Outlook–Nvidia 27 年規劃不變，28 年 DC 佈建放緩 
NVIDIA Vera Rubin 上季貢獻 200 億美元營收，估 ODM 將於 4Q26~1Q27 初認列，我們估 
4Q26 VR 出貨約 2,000 櫃，目前上游瓶頸已逐步改善，估 1Q27 VR 將大幅季增至 1.5 萬櫃以
上，2Q27 再雙位數季增，Rubin Ultra 預計於 2H27 推出，27 年 NVIDIA 9 萬櫃預估不變。
2027 年 ASIC 機櫃出貨將超越 GPU，受惠 Google 擴大外賣 TPU，估 TPU 出貨將逾 10 萬櫃，
AWS Trainium 出貨年增逾 35%，ASIC 在更有性價比且產能足以因應下，27 年機櫃數將正式超
越 GPU。我們預期 27 年在監管制尚未普及且審查完成資料中心加速建置下，1H27 ODM 營運
將持續創高。然 2028 年因審查將趨嚴、使 CSP 承擔更高電力成本，整體資本支出將放緩至年增
雙位數。受制於 BIS 對中國算力取得範圍擴張及 2028 年資料中心成本增加，28 年起 AI 高速成
長不確定性將增加。 
Investment Summary 
2028 年美國監管趨嚴使資料中心成本上升，將可能影響 2028 年 AI 資本支出波動加劇，近期 BIS
將算力租賃管制擴大至中系企業客戶，未來 Neocloud 可能將面臨整併潮，我們認為 27 年 ODM
營收獲利將持續創高，然 28 年起受客戶佈建資料中心不確定性增加，而面臨獲利年增幅下修風
險，鴻海本季獲利創高並大幅優於預期，營益率即使 AI 高單價機櫃加入下，ASIC 成長增速擴張，
預期營收將持續季增，評價處於今、明年獲利低檔，維持買進評等。

2 
BIS 收緊管制，大型 CSP 及中型 Neocloud 影響相對低 
美國商務部將 AI 管制延伸至海外資料中心，過去中國企業受晶片管制，然透過租賃海外算力訓
練自有模型，美國商務部將禁止海外資料中心將算力租賃給中國公司(嚴格審查租賃企業終端是否
為中國母公司)，預計至 2027/2 將為過渡期。

此次較過去管制主要差異在於取消 AI Diffusion 國家分級制度，重點管制區域為新加坡、馬來西
亞、印尼、泰國、巴西等，未來資料中心設於上述國家將面臨嚴格審視。以東南亞資料中心為主
要佈局廠商可能將成為風險地區，然觀察算力價格短期並未大幅崩跌，相關資料中心算力可能將
透過轉單至歐、美等地緩解。

觀察阿里巴巴及騰訊財報會議，隨海外算力監管驅嚴，中系 CSP 將降低全球算力租賃比重，阿里
巴巴下修 28 財年雲端營收成長至 30~45%，亦可能下修 2032 年全球資料中心容量>20GW 目
標，然隨自有平頭哥（T-Head）晶片擴產，預期 2028 年雲端營收回穩，YoY+50%。騰訊部分
預付款算力合約將取消，未來季度將出現預付款回沖。騰訊並未下修雲端營收展望，主要基於國
產晶片及模型推論效率改善，皆將支撐雲端營收成長，估 27 年資本支出維持高檔，28 年後逐步
下滑。兩家公司皆反映中國 CSP 在海外算力取得受限將使其轉向中國國產晶片。

綜合以上，後續在大型 CSP 及 Neocloud 等將更嚴格審查客戶終端資金來源，我們認為鴻海、廣
達、緯穎等主要客戶為大型 CSP 廠商影響較低，主要觀察美國 CSP 全球資料中心發電量分佈，
歐、美地區合計高達 80~88%，美國當地資料中心審查客戶相對嚴格，降低中系企業直接租賃算
力機率。預期大型 CSP 受影響程度較低。

圖 1：CSP 全球資料中心用電量

資料來源：各公司，國泰證期研究部

### Page 3

3

Neocloud 數量龐大且全球分布多元，DELL、SMCI 及技嘉、華碩等以 Neocloud 為主要客戶，
觀察中大型 Neocloud 如 Coreweave 及 Nebius 在美國、英國外的營收分別 6%及 9.8%，與大
型 CSP 資料中心分布型態相仿，且上述業者在手訂單中，約有半數以上為美國大型 CSP 客戶，
CSP 包下 Neocloud 算力再將雲端算力配合自身軟體轉售給客戶使用，終端出租多已受
Hyperscaler 管制。然小型 CSP(規模低於 0.5 億美元)過去不是主要管制目標，亦有部分算力外
租給中系客戶，後續可能受到中系客戶撤銷訂單而造成營收大幅下修。未來小型 CSP 算力將有可
能被中大型 CSP 收購，進行全球算力整併，大者恆大。觀察目前 B300 租賃價格如下圖 2 仍維持
高檔，預期短期釋出算力將陸續被消化，全球需求強勁下，算力價格依舊可維持高檔。

圖 2：算力價格尚處高檔

資料來源：NEBIUS，國泰證期研究部

Nvidia Roadmap 2027 年不變 
Nvidia 本次財報重點在於，(1)上修 FY2028 營收成長至 YoY+75%，顯示在 1H27 前多數營收皆
已確認，FY2028 毛利率將回升至 72~73%，反映新產品出貨貢獻放大且單價明顯拉升。(2)公司
表示 Hyperscaler 導入 Vera Rubin 較 Blackwell 更快，上季貢獻金額達 200 億美元，預期終端
出貨櫃數約三千櫃，ODM 將陸續於 4Q26~1Q27 初期認列，Rubin Ultra 則維持 2027 下半年
推出，時程並未遞延。(3)ACIE 營收 475 億美元，YoY+195%，NVIDIA 未來將選擇性支持
Neocloud 融資計畫達 320 億美元，聚焦土地、電力與資料中心建物，Neocloud 須有足夠股權
或債務融資、完成設備部署及驗收，相關承諾才會生效。

綜上，研究部維持今、明年在 AI GPU 出貨成長 200%、47%，估今年 Blackwell、Rubin 比重分
別為 98%、2%，預期 4Q26 起 Vera Rubin 總量將攀升至近 2000 櫃，大型 CSP 的鴻海、緯創
為主要出貨廠商。AMD 部分，MI450 系列在公版及 META 版於 2027 年合計達萬櫃以上，客戶
包含 OpenAI、Microsoft 及 Oracle 等大型雲端廠商，27 年 AMD 於 AI 市場市佔率將首度拉升
至雙位數。綜合以上，4Q26 ODM AI 伺服器及一般伺服器出貨量小幅季增，營收也因產品單價
提升而將個位數季增，營收續創高趨勢不變。

### Page 4

4

2028 年全美資料中心佈建將受德州審查趨嚴遞延，看好 ASIC 供應鏈 
美國德州是全美電力資源最豐沛地區，根據研調資料顯示，2027 年預計全美資料中心審查已有
超越 75%以上已動工，此部分資料中心預期將加速建置，德州期中選舉後已開放空污許可，德州
環境監管機構 TCEQ 也陸續核發資料中心許可審查，預期 2027 年已審核過的資料中心將加速建
置，也將帶動 ODM 出貨維持新高水準。然預期 2028 年因德州佔新增資料中心比重的 25~30%，
考量德州審查可能趨嚴，導致 CSP 需負擔電力成本擴增，預期 2028 年資本支出可能將因此受調
整，五大 CSP 資本支出年增幅度將縮小至雙位數。

研究部維持看好 ASIC 成長增速，我們預期 2027 年 TPU 在外賣增加下，總體出貨數量將達
800~900 萬顆，出貨櫃數將擴張至 10 萬櫃以上，因數量翻倍成長，且預期供應商數量將擴張至
4~5 家供應商，以穩定供給來源。估 AWS Trainium 今、明年出貨顆數將分別 220 萬顆、300 萬
顆，YoY+36%，因液冷比重增加，單價明顯提升，預期 2027 年兩大 ASIC 機櫃總量將超越 GPU
機櫃，單櫃 TDP 也低於 NVIDIA 產品，Google Ironwood TCO 較 GB200 約低 44%，其成本
優勢將使 ASIC 在產能取得下，更具競爭力。看好 ASIC 相關供應鏈包含聯發科、世芯-KY 及鴻
海、緯穎等。

圖 3：27 年 ASIC 櫃數將大於 GPU

資料來源：國泰證期研究部整理

4Q26 伺服器廠營收續創新高 
3Q26 ODM 營收優於預期，因缺料改善使一般伺服器及 AI 出貨維持高檔，鴻海及技嘉營益率皆
優於預期，獲利再創新高。預期第四季在缺料持續好轉下，短期 VR 出貨櫃數約 2,000 櫃內，略
低於預期，主要仍是新品面臨生產瓶頸，而非客戶砍單風險，台廠 4Q26 營收預期在新舊產品交
接期，仍可維持季增，毛利率小幅季減。預期 1Q27 起 Vera Rubin 出貨將跳升至 1.5 萬櫃以上
水準，GB300 估自 2Q26 起占比將低於 Vera Rubin。

Document 491: ここが知りたい『NISAとiDeCoの税制優遇』

Source PDF: all report pdf/ここが知りたい『NISAとiDeCoの税制優遇』.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-18
Pages: 1
Status: ok

### Page 1

NISAとiDeCoの税制優遇

資産運用調査部　シニア研究員　重原 正明（しげはら まさあき）
　国民の資産形成を促進する仕組みの一つに、税制優遇
がある。NISA（少額投資非課税制度）の投資限度額や
iDeCo（個人型確定拠出年金）の拠出枠をどう使おうかと
考えている人も多いであろう。だが、同じ税制優遇でも、
例えばNISAとiDeCoとではその中身が違うことは、あま
り意識されていないようである。以下、NISAとiDeCoに
代表される２つの税制優遇制度のパターンについて解説
する。
　NISAの場合は、投資する金額に関して税額控除や所得
控除といった税優遇は与えられない。所得税などを差し引
かれた後の所得をもとに投資を行う。ただしその投資に
よって得られた収益は非課税となり、受け取るときは元本
と投資収益分を合わせた受取額全体が非課税となる。
　最初に課税（Taxed：T）され、運用収益は課税免除
（Exempt：E）、受取時も非課税（E）ということで、このよう
な税優遇制度はTEEと表されることがある。制度としては
税優遇となる投資収益のみを把握するだけで済むので、
実施は比較的容易と考えられる。
　TEEの制度としては、NISAの他に、障がい者などが利
用できるマル優（預貯金）、特別マル優（国債・地方債）の
制度がある。
　これに対してiDeCoの場合は、拠出する金額（掛金）の
分については所得税や住民税がかからない。掛金が税法
上の「小規模企業共済等掛金控除」という控除項目とな
り、税法上の所得金額から差し引かれるからである。また、
掛金から生じた投資収益にも、収益が生じた時点では税
金がかからない。
税制優遇といっても2種類ある
NISAは受取時非課税（TEE）
iDeCoは拠出時非課税（EET）
　一方、iDeCoの資産を引き出す（受け取る）際には、受取
金額全体が課税の対象となる。ただし60歳以上になって
から、年金で受け取ったり、一時金で受け取ったりする場
合には、それぞれ公的年金等控除や退職所得控除の対象
となり、税の優遇が受けられる。
　iDeCoは拠出時非課税（E）、運用益非課税（E）、受取時
課税（T）ということでEETと表されることがある。EETの制
度としては、他に厚生年金や国民年金といった公的年金制
度、企業年金（DBおよびDC）、国民年金基金などがある。
割と制度として確立し、その分制約が強いものが多いよう
に思われる。
　TEEの制度の場合、実際には引き出す時にならないと
税優遇の恩恵を受けることができない。EETであれば投
資をする時点で税優遇のメリットを受けることができる。
また、EETでは課税される時期が一般的に所得の減る高
齢期になるため、税率が低くなる可能性もある。
　一方、EETの制度の場合、引出時には元本だけでなく運
用益分についても税金の対象となる。従って、運用益の課
税を単に繰り延べるだけとなる可能性もある（ただし先に
述べたような控除制度での優遇がある場合もある）。TEE
の場合には運用益には税金は全くかからない。
　他の制度との関係もあり、NISAのようなTEEとiDeCo
のようなEETと、どちらが有利かは一概には判断できな
い。大切なのは、税制優遇というだけで飛びつくのではな
く、その中身を知って活用することではないだろうか。税
制優遇制度を正しく活用することで、読者のウェルビーイ
ングが高まることを願う。
（注 ： 本稿は制度の解説を行ったものであり、税務会計上のアドバイスを
行うものではありません）
TEEとEETのメリット・デメリット
各制度の内容を知ったうえで税制優遇の活用を
9 第一ライフ研レポート 2026.12
ここが知りたい
ここが知りたい

景気は弱い動き
中国景気は弱い動きが続いているが、改善の兆しもみられる。2026年7～9月期の実質GDP成長率は前年比＋4.5%
だった。前期（前年比＋4.3%）から伸びが拡大し、1～9月累計で同＋4.6%と政府目標の「4.5～5%」の範囲内で推移した。
ただし、10月の製造業PMIは49.3と前月から低下し、好不況の境目である50を2カ月ぶりに下回った。構成指数をみると、
生産と新規受注の低下が全体を押し下げた。非製造業PMIも49.7と低下し、50を下回った。

通じて下落した。業種別指数は、2025年以降の株価上昇を牽引してきた情報や通信の下落が大きかった。

期連続で前年割れとなった。

国慶節の人出は増加も、財布のひもは依然固い
中国の建国記念日である国慶節（10/1）に伴う大型連休（10/1～7）の1日当たり国内旅行者数は、前年比＋6.3%と増加
した（左図）。休日日数は前年より1日短かったものの、今年は中秋節の連休が9月末にあり、間で休暇を取れば最大13連
休となる日並びだった。このため、海外旅行需要も高まり、連休中の1日当たり出入国者数は、前年比＋8.1%と増加した。
もっとも、連休中の1人1日当たり旅行消費額は2024年以降伸び悩んでおり、今年は前年比－1.9%と2年連続で減少した
（右図）。旅行需要は底堅いものの、消費者の節約志向は依然として根強い。

Document 636: 台股杠杆退潮规模创30年之最，高盛下调评级并削减目标点位

Source PDF: all report pdf/台股杠杆退潮规模创30年之最，高盛下调评级并削减目标点位.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

台股上周五融资余额单⽇骤降 490 亿元台币，创 1996 年有统计以来最⼤单⽇降幅。⾼盛随
即将中国台湾评级从 “ 增持 ” 下调⾄ “ 中性 ” ，并⼤幅削减⽬标点位，理由是 AI 资本⽀出与市场
⾼度集中等⻛险。与此同时， 53% 的股票融资维持率已低于 130% 追缴保证⾦⻔槛，被迫去
杠杆⻛险仍⾼。
摘要：
中国台湾股市正经历⼀场去杠杆⻛暴。上周五单⽇融资余额骤降 490 亿元台币，创下近 30 年最⼤
单⽇降幅，⾼盛随即将台湾股市评级从 " 增持 " 下调⾄ " 中性 " ，并⼤幅削减⽬标点位。
中国台湾基准加权指数上周重挫 17.5% ，创 1991 年以来最⼤单周跌幅。周⼀指数反弹收涨
2.16% ，但市场情绪依然脆弱。
据彭博报道，上市股票融资余额上周五单⽇⼤减 490 亿元台币（约 15 亿美元），创下彭博⾃ 1996
年开始统计以来的单⽇最⼤降幅。融资余额⽬前已较 10 ⽉创下的约 6,530 亿元台币纪录⾼点下降
逾 30% 。
⾼盛驻⾹港策略师 Alvin So 在周六发布的研究报告中直⾔： " 散⼾杠杆已⼤幅下降，但追缴保证

⾦和被迫去杠杆的⻛险仍然偏⾼。 " 他指出，整体市场融资维持率已降⾄ 132% ，接近 130% 的追
缴保证⾦⻔槛， " ⽬前已有 53% 的股票低于这⼀⽔平 " 。
⾼盛下调评级，⽬标点位⼤幅缩⽔
⾼盛在 11 ⽉ 21 ⽇发布的报告中，将中国台湾在亚洲区域配置中的评级从 " 增持 " （ Overweight ）
下调⾄ " 中性 " （ Marketweight ），并将 2027 年底加权指数⽬标点位从 57,500 点下调⾄ 50,000 点。
以上周五指数⽔平约 36,034 点计算， 50,000 点的⽬标意味着约 40% 的潜在上涨空间，但⾼盛同
时指出，当前台股 40% 的隐含波动率⼤幅压缩了⻛险调整后的回报吸引⼒。
报告写道： " 强劲的盈利增⻓（预计 2027/2028 年分别增⻓ 32%/25% ）仍具⽀撑，但 AI 资本⽀出
⻛险、市场⾼度集中、相对估值溢价以及波动率居⾼不下，共同削弱了⻛险调整后的回报吸引
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⼒。 "
⾼盛同时维持 " 优质科技股 " 作为核⼼持仓，并辅以 " 股息⽀撑的 AI 多元化配置 " 作为战术性补充。
外资持续净卖，散⼾逆势接盘
外资卖压是本轮下跌的核⼼驱动⼒。据⾼盛研报，外资（ QFII ）上周净卖出台股现货 58 亿美
元，其中台积电单周遭净卖出 38 亿美元。年初⾄今，外资累计净卖出台股约 950 亿美元，台积电
⼀只股票就占了约 740 亿美元。
与此同时，散⼾投资者逆势买⼊。数据显⽰， ETF 上周吸引约 48 亿美元净流⼊，其中宽基被动
型 ETF 流⼊ 25 亿美元，股息型 ETF 流⼊ 9 亿美元，杠杆型及主动型 ETF 各流⼊约 7 亿美元。散⼾在
融资⼤幅去化的同时，仍维持净买⼊姿态。
然⽽，散⼾的杠杆敞⼝依然不容忽视。数据显⽰，杠杆 ETF 规模仍⾼达 110 亿美元，占市场⾃由
流通股的 0.8% 。 Alvin So 在报告中警告，这⼀敞⼝在市场持续下跌的情况下，仍构成潜在的被
迫去杠杆⻛险。
卖空限制层层加码
⾯对持续下跌，中国台湾本⽉已三度收紧卖空规则。

中国台湾周六宣布，全⾯禁⽌低于前⼀交易⽇收盘价的卖空操作，涵盖融券卖空及借券卖空，
新规于 11 ⽉ 23 ⽇起⽣效。这是本⽉第三次出台卖空限制措施。
此前，已于 11 ⽉ 9 ⽇将借券卖空的⽇内上限从 30% 下调⾄ 3% （以 30 ⽇平均成交量为基准），并将
融券保证⾦要求从 90% 提⾼⾄ 130% 。 11 ⽉ 20 ⽇再度收紧，将上限进⼀步压缩⾄ 1% ，并规定当
⽇跌幅超过 3.5% 的股票，次⽇不得在前收盘价以下卖空。最新措施还将融券保证⾦要求进⼀步
提⾼⾄ 150% ，并暂停受理证券商申请上市新认沽权证。
盈利基本⾯尚稳，但估值溢价制约空间
⾼盛研报指出，台股盈利基本⾯仍具⽀撑。中国台湾第三季度整体盈利同⽐增⻓ 71% ， 2027 年
预期每股盈利共识上调 0.3% 。科技板块预计在 2027/2028 年分别实现 32%/25% 的盈利增⻓。
然⽽，估值层⾯的压⼒不容忽视。数据显⽰， MSCI 台湾指数当前 12 个⽉远期市盈率为 12.7 倍
（低于历史均值 1.3 个标准差）， 24 个⽉远期市盈率为 10.0 倍（低于历史均值 2.2 个标准差）。尽管

绝对估值已有所回落，但台湾科技股相对全球科技同业仍存在 21% 的市盈率溢价（剔除台积电
后为 15% ），处于历史⾼位，限制了其在全球配置中的相对吸引⼒。
报告同时指出，科技板块在中国台湾整体盈利中的占⽐已从 2010 年代初的约 50% 升⾄ 2027 年预
期的约 85%，使台股对 AI 及半导体基本⾯的敏感度⼤幅提升。
散⼾杠杆与外资持仓双⾼，去化压⼒犹存
⾼盛研报揭⽰了当前市场结构的深层⽭盾：外资⼤规模卖出的同时，散⼾和 ETF 持续接盘，但双
⽅的持仓均处于历史⾼位，去化压⼒尚未充分释放。
外资⽅⾯，尽管年初⾄今已净卖出约 950 亿美元，外资在台积电的持股⽐例仍⾼达 69% ，在其他
科技股的持股⽐例也维持在 46% 左右。该⾏指出，对冲基⾦仍⼤幅超配亚洲信息科技板块，若 AI
不确定性和市场波动持续，存在进⼀步减仓空间。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Document 674: 家計のおカネは株式から国債へ 日銀の利上げの影響

Source PDF: all report pdf/家計のおカネは株式から国債へ 日銀の利上げの影響.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-18
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026年 11月 18日(水)

1/2 
家計のおカネは株式から国債へ 日銀の利上げの影響    
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・昨日の当レポート「中央銀行と闘うな 利上げ局面で株式は弱い 」では、最近のＡＩ・半導
体株を中心とする世界的な株価調整は、Ｆｅｄを中心とする主要中銀の利上げが重要な理由
であると述べた。事実、米10月ＣＰＩが発表される直前にウォラー理事が50bpの利上げに言
及し、Ｆｅｄの利上げ観測が高まったことで、株式に対する逆風は強くなった。その点、Ｆ
ｅｄの合意形成に影響を与えることも多いウィリアムズ・ＮＹ連銀総裁が11月17日に50bpの
利上げに否定的な見解を示したことは安心材料である。同氏は「ここ数カ月の政策運営はう
まく機能していると思うが、インフレ率を適時、かつ持続的に２％に戻すためにまだやるべ
き仕事は残っている」「このところの経済指標で米経済が底堅さを維持し⁠ていることが明確
に示されており、労働市場も堅調だが、インフレは望ましい方向に向かっていない」としつ
つも「金融政策による劇的な対応が必要な状況ではない」との見解を示した。ここでいう
「やるべき仕事」は12月ＦＯＭＣにおける25bpの追加利上げ、「劇的な対応」は50bp利上げ
を指していると考えられる。11月入り後、堅調な雇用統計と強いＣＰＩが重なったことで、
追加利上げが正当化された一方、賃金上昇率の鈍化や住宅市況の軟化といった状況が継続
し、インフレ加速の証左に乏しいなか、やはり50bpの利上げは唐突な印象が拭えない。Ａ
Ｉ・半導体株の急落によって金融環境が引き締まったことも50bp利上げの必要性を減じたと
考えられる。 
・日経平均株価は先行き 12ヶ月 72,000円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPYは先行き 12ヶ月 155円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を 12月に 1.5％とした後、27年末までに 2.25％とするだろう 
・FEDはFF金利（誘導目標上限）を、12月に4.25％へ引き上げるだろう

### Page 2

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
2/2
・この間、日銀の利上げ継続も株価下押し要因として効いている。金融環境は引き続き緩和的
であるが、日銀が言うとおり緩和度合いが低下しているのは事実であり、これも日本株の頭
を抑えていると考えられる。身近な例として「個人向け国債」の販売好調があろう。財務省
によれば、10月募集分（11月発行）は2.03兆円に達したという。販売全体の65％を占めた固
定５年の利率は税引き前2.39％であり、この絶対水準に惹かれた家計が多かったと推察され
る。2024年３月にマイナス金利を解除して以降、しばらくの間は金利の絶対水準が「雀の
涙」程度であり、個人向け国債の販売額に大きな変化はなかった。しかしながら、政策金利
が2026年６月に１％となった後、９月には1.25％となり、家計が金利を実感できるようにな

ったことで、非線形な影響がみられつつある。個人向け国債の販売増加それ自体は、インフ
レ下における個人の資産形成において重要な役割を果たすものであり歓迎すべきであろう。
一方、株式市場に対してはネガティブな面がある。というのも、かつて株価の急落する局面
では個人投資家による押し目買いが相場を支える構図が一般的であったが、そうした構図が
変化した可能性があるためだ。象徴的だったのは日経平均株価が前週末比5855円（9.3％）も
下落した11月２～６日の投資部門別売買動向。東証・名証２市場の現物取引で個人投資家は
4,802億円の売り越しを記録した（売り越しは３週連続）。10月中旬以降の株価下落局面で、
家計はリスクをとって株式の下値を拾うよりも、安全に利回りを取れる個人向け国債を選好
した格好となる。従来、株式相場に流入してきたおカネが（個人向け）国債へ流れたこと
で、相場を下支えする力が弱くなった印象である。日銀の利上げが株式市場に染み渡ってい
ることを物語る。

▋ 川習會科技議題高舉輕放 
9 月川習會前市場高度關注美中針對晶片
或尖端科技議題，進行技術和供應鏈管制等
相關主題的討論，然而最終僅將 AI 重新定
義為超級智慧(Super Intelligence)、設立AI
雙邊對話管道等風險控管議題。美中在科技
敏感議題上避重就輕，藉此換取貿易戰休兵
延長至 2027 年初。

美國科技新聞網站 The Information 報導，
中國政府可能允許字節跳動及阿里巴巴等
大型中國科技企業購買一款輝達供高階 AI
推論與工作站應用設計的新晶片。該晶片並
非專門針對大型資料中心訓練的高階 AI 晶
片，而未受美國現行出口管制規範，在中國
方面雖尚無採購數量、時間表及審批標準的
具體訊息，不過若中國政府放行企業採購管
制，為大型科技企業提供及時的硬體支撐，
或可視為美中晶片管制措施上具政策彈性。

▋ 美中競逐發展超級智慧 
美中超級智慧熱線旨在建立直接溝通管道，
以應對駭客攻擊、國家安全疑慮或失控等潛
在問題，但若回顧過往美中兩國熱線電話的
歷史，因美中文化或戰略歧異(美方傾向即
時通話避免衝突升級，中方高度集權使下屬
沒有取得最高領導人授權下不能表態)而使
雙方在危機時刻並未能透過電話產生立即
性的有效溝通。市場期待美中雙方溝通仍有
待長期磨合並改採更可行(如文字溝通方式)
的對話機制。(圖二) 
此外，市場分析美國可望於明年初步建立自
主稀土供應，屆時可能恢復對中國施壓，並
將執行嚴格管制和封鎖，美中將持續在尖端
科技上競爭，並各自拉攏盟友推動全球形成
兩套不同的科技和供應鏈體系。即便美中在
超級智慧發展上看似聯手風險控管又同時
互相掣肘下，仍不改產業長線發展方向，且
在各國普遍加碼投入 AI 發展下，瑞銀預估
全球 AI 資本支出今年上看 9,980 億美元，
明年支出規模將不斷攀升。(圖三)

圖二：歷史上美中熱線未達成即時有效溝通

▋ 中國官方力推科技自主 
上半年中國全國人大四中全會批准並公佈
實施第十五個五年規劃綱要，將高水平科技
自立自強列為首要核心目標，規劃全社會研
發經費年均成長率維持超過 7%，以及先前
提出的新質生產力(以科技創新為主導的高
階生產力推動產業發展)均顯示政府推動科
技自主的戰略方向，而美國在科技上的掣肘
措施，更加刺激中國官方加速建立本土自主
可控的 AI 生態與供應鏈替代的政策方向，
以突破美國的晶片與技術禁令，預期中國科
技產業發展仍將持續獲政策或補貼支持。

在 AI 基礎建設計畫方面，未來五年內中國
規劃編列 5 兆元人民幣投資於新型電網，
預計將拉動特高壓、智慧電網及配電網升級，
還有投入 2 兆元人民幣整合全國數據中心
資源，以 實現算力統一調度等一系列基礎建
設計畫，可望支援未來中國城市中自動駕駛、
無人機和智慧城市的龐大算力需求。

受到美國持續收緊高階晶片出口管制，此次
建設中國官方明確指定至少 80%必須採用
國產晶片與技術設備，以提高中國 AI 晶片
自給率，中國本土半導體與 AI 晶片產業將
成為最大政策受益者，華為、寒武紀等中國
本土算力晶片可望加速技術迭代與商業化。
高盛預期中國國產AI 晶片出貨量將從2025
年 110 萬顆、2026 年 240 萬顆、2027 年
480 萬顆，並攀升至 2030 年 1,100 萬顆，
市場規模將有望從人民幣 940 億元成長至
人民幣 6,460 億元。此外，中國伺服器、散
熱溫控等上下游產業鏈，以及電網相關建設
廠商亦將受惠。(圖四)

圖四：中國國產 AI 晶片出貨量看增 
資料來源：GS，2026/9/26

▋ 結論 
美中在科技與政治上的角力，不但沒有阻礙
AI 產業的發展，反而使競爭白熱化，成為推
動中國加速技術突破與產業擴張的強力催
化劑。為爭奪未來數十年的科技主導權，中
美雙方傾注國力提供政策支援或政府補貼
推動 AI 發展，民間企業如微軟、Google、
Meta、百度、騰訊、阿里等科技巨頭提出
史無前例的資金規模投入 AI 研發與算力基
礎設施。在 晶片和演算法軍備競賽中，中國
除了在發達的製造業領域，還有自動駕駛、
無人機等應用場景中全方位加速 AI 深度融
入，可望挹注中國科技業長線增長動能。

Document 846: 阻止美联储加息的最快方式：美股“以死相逼”

Source PDF: all report pdf/阻止美联储加息的最快方式：美股“以死相逼”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-22
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

美银⾸席投资策略师 Michael Hartnett 在本周最新的 Flow Show 指出，阻⽌美联储继续加息的最
快⽅式，是让股市回吐 2026 年的全部涨幅。这⼀⽬标位对应标普 500 指数 6,845 点，⽽该指数上
周五收于 6,925 点，距离触发仅剩 80 点、约 1.2% 的空间。在连续六周维持 " 避险 " ⽴场后，
Hartnett 的框架正从预测变为市场现实。
上周美股录得 " 解放⽇ " 以来最差单周表现。标普 500 单周下跌 8.4% ，连续第七个交易⽇⾛低并跌
破 200 ⽇均线，进⼊技术性回调；纳斯达克 100 较 10 ⽉纪录⾼点回落 19.8% ，逼近熊市边缘；
VIX ⼀度突破 39 。据 Hartnett 统计，标普 500 年内涨幅已从⼀周前的 10.9% 收窄⾄ 1.2% 。
市场已经开始替美联储 " 做⼯作 " 。上周五盘中，美联储理事 Waller 主张的 50 个基点 " 震撼式 " 加息
被移出选项， 1 ⽉加息概率降⾄ 60% 以下， 10 年期美债收益率回落⾄ 5% 以下。但美联储官员的

表态并未松动：理事 Cook 称⾦融条件仍过于宽松，费城联储的 Paulson 对连续加息持开放态
度，会议纪要显⽰⼏乎所有官员都认为需要进⼀步收紧。
Hartnett 给出的判断是：如果美联储仍是华尔街的 " 好朋友 " ，那么信⽤利差⾛阔与股市下跌将提
前终结加息周期，触发条件是标普跌破 6,845 点、 IG CDX 升破 80 个基点；如果美联储在中期选
举后转为真正的通胀⽃⼠，硬着陆⻛险将上升，市场最早要到明年 3 ⽉ 18 ⽇或 4 ⽉ 28 ⽇的议息会
议才能定价 " 利率⻅顶 " 。
" 停⽌美联储的最快⽅式 " ：两个数字与两道⻔槛
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

Hartnett 将本轮逻辑概括为⼀句话： " 阻⽌美联储的最快⽅式是股市回吐全部年内涨幅。 " 标普
500 2025 年收于 6,845 点，这⼀⽔平成为他判断政策转向的第⼀道⻔槛。
另⼀道⻔槛在信⽤市场。投资级 CDX 上周突破 80 个基点，现⾦投资级利差单周⾛阔约 10 个基
点，为 2025 年 5 ⽉以来最阔。股债两端的压⼒，构成了他所说 " 市场替美联储做事 " 的传导链条。
⾼盛对机制给出相同判断。据⾼盛观点，股市下跌是唯⼀能阻⽌加息的因素；⾼盛的 Tony
Pasquariello 上周五则反问，美联储是否即将 " 动真格 " 。
分歧在于⼒度。 Hartnett 认为 6,845 点⾜以让美联储收⼿；⽽质疑者指出，⼀个通胀⽬标已连续
67 个⽉未达标、案头摆着三份偏热 CPI 数据、且刚刚⽬睹选⺠因物价惩罚执政党的美联储，需要
的远不⽌标普全年零涨幅。 2018 年的经验是 20% 的跌幅，⽽当时的美联储并没有通胀问题。
美元跑赢标普，⻩⾦⽐特币沦为 " 仓位 "
Hartnett 的年度表现榜单呈现出清晰的⻛险资产收缩：原油涨 44.9% ，美元涨 5.7% ，美股涨

4.8% ，全球股市涨 4.4% ，现⾦涨 3.3% ；⾼收益债跌 1.2% ，投资级债跌 3.0% ，政府债跌 3.9% ，
⻩⾦跌 15.1% ，⽐特币跌 29.5% 。
⼀周之内，美股年内涨幅从 10.9% 降⾄ 4.8% （截⾄上周四），纳斯达克全年转跌。原油的领先幅
度连续第三周收窄，从 54.0% 降⾄ 44.9% ；两⼤ " 货币贬值对冲 " 资产从 " 难看 " 变为 " 更难看 " ：⻩⾦
从 -10.4% 扩⼤⾄ -15.1% ，⽐特币从 -18.9% 扩⼤⾄ -29.5% 。唯⼀上涨的是美元，从 4.9% 升⾄
5.7% ，意味着美元在 2026 年已跑赢标普 500 ， " 除了美元什么都买 " 的交易宣告失效。
截⾄上周五收盘，情况进⼀步恶化：标普年内仅涨 1.2% ，⻩⾦下探 3,500 美元，⽐特币跌破
60,000 美元，创 2024 年 9 ⽉以来新低。据 The Market Ear 此前提⽰，⻩⾦的去杠杆远未结束。
Hartnett ⽤⼀张美元与美联储政策拐点的对照图，解释他为何在意 " 哪⼀个美联储 " 会出现。过去

⼀年每⼀次政策摆动，都对应着某类资产的⾼点：去年 10 ⽉ 29 ⽇的鸽派降息对应⽐特币⾼点，
今年 1 ⽉ 30 ⽇特朗普提名 Warsh 对应⻩⾦⾼点， 6 ⽉ 17 ⽇ Warsh 在 FOMC 上的鸽派失误对应半导
体与韩国 KOSPI ⾼点， 9 ⽉ 16 ⽇的鹰派加息则对应铜价⾼点、投资级利差低点与美元升势的起
点。

### Page 3

反过来说，加息恐慌⻅顶的信号是上述⾛势的镜像：美元⻅顶，⽐特币、⻩⾦与 KOSPI ⻅底。
⽬前的状况是四项全部落空 —— 美元录得 2025 年 4 ⽉以来最强单周收盘，⽐特币连跌七周，⻩⾦
回到 2025 年 9 ⽉⽔平， KOSPI 跌破 5,000 点。唯⼀的转机迹象是上周五避险资⾦开始从美元转向
⽇元。
3100 亿美元逢低买⼊， 3% 押注硬着陆
Hartnett 本周第⼆个关键词是 " 清算事件 " （ clearing event ） —— 他对这个词的泛滥明显不耐。其
仓位数据恰好解释了原因：没有⼈完成清算，就不会有清算事件。
美银 11 ⽉基⾦经理调查显⽰，仅 3% 的投资者预期 " 硬着陆 " ， 50% 预期 " 软着陆 " ， 42% 预期 " 不着
陆 " ；现⾦仓位处于 3.6% 的极低⽔平；资产配置为多股票、空债券；过去四周流⼊股票基⾦与
ETF 的资⾦达创纪录的 3,100 亿美元。 Hartnett 的结论是：价格已经投降，仓位尚未投降，在后
者出现前不宜给出全⾯买⼊信号。

分歧确实存在。⾼盛 Prime 账本显⽰，对冲基⾦净杠杆处于五年多来最低，低于 " 解放⽇ " 时的低
点，科技股卖出速度为⼗年最快，个股做空规模为 2022 年 6 ⽉以来最⼤。但对冲基⾦并⾮主要持
仓者 —— 承担损失的是 ETF ，⽽ ETF 投资者上周仍在买⼊ AI 股的下跌。
美银⽜熊指标从 7.4 降⾄ 7.1 ，原因是新兴市场债与垃圾债⼤幅流出、⾼收益债与银⾏次级债利差
⾛阔、市场宽度恶化以及期货仓位转空。该指标仍更接近 " 卖出 " ⽽⾮ " 恐慌 " ：全球市场宽度处于
第 18 百分位，股票资⾦流处于第 99 百分位，基⾦经理仓位固定在第 100 百分位。
债市没有减压阀：⻓债或连续第六年负回报
本轮抛售中，债券⼤部分时间未能提供缓冲。 Hartnett 指出， 30 年期美债正⾛向连续第六年负价
格回报，为 1963 ⾄ 1969 年越南战争时期以来最⻓纪录。截⾄其撰写时， 30 年期收益率距近期⾼
点仅 25 个基点，⽽纳斯达克已跌 15% 、银⾏股跌 16% 、原油跌 22% 。他的核⼼担忧是：下⼀轮

衰退中收益率上⾏⽽⾮下⾏的⻛险更⼤，因为债务⽔平过⾼。
上周五 30 年期美债收益率收于 5.31% ，单周下⾏ 18 个基点，减压阀似乎开了⼀条缝。 Citadel
Securities 认为收益率与增⻓之间出现了创纪录的 3.5 个标准差缺⼝。但 Hartnett 笔记内部存在张
⼒：第⼀⻚说衰退中收益率上⾏的⻛险最⼤，第三⻚的硬着陆剧本却以配置者买⼊债券开场；
⽽他上周还说，在收益率达到 5.5% ⾄ 6.5% 之前⽆⼈愿意碰美债 —— 三者⽆法同时成⽴。

### Page 4

软着陆剧本： 7,000 点以下是买点
Hartnett 给出的软着陆路径是：若春季前通胀回落、就业稳定、⽣产率提升得到确认，盈利增速
维持在 10% 以上，则标普 7,000 点以下即为买点 —— 上周五收盘已低于该⽔平 75 点。届时 " 宽度
⽜市 " 重启，未参与 AI ⾏情的板块不再昂贵：等权重标普市盈率 16 倍，中盘股 15 倍（ 30 年均值 16
倍），美国以外股票 12 倍（均值 13 倍）。
顺序同样重要。他的建议是先保住资本，等待恐慌显性化，待叙事从加息转向降息，再买⼊周
期性的 "Cs" ：加密货币、芯⽚、铜、消费与公寓地产。
估值矩阵显⽰，标普 7,338 点对应 408 美元前瞻每股收益的 18 倍，隐含 16% 的盈利增⻓，接近市
场⼀致预期。上周五的 6,925 点对应 17 倍，或按 10% 增⻓下的 387 美元计算为 17.9 倍。若盈利仅
增⻓ 5% （ 370 美元）并给予 16 倍估值，矩阵给出的点位是 5,913 点；⾼盛的 Brian Garrett 认为若

完全重演 2018 年四季度，终点在 6,294 点，恰为同⼀盈利的 17 倍。两家机构、两种⽅法，坏情形
的落点⾼度接近。
硬着陆剧本：从 " 繁荣循环 " 到 " 末⽇循环 "
Hartnett 押注的是坏情形。他认为 " 硬着陆 " 概率被低估，市场对 " 繁荣 " 的⼀致预期过⾼ —— 名义
GDP 增⻓ 5% ⾄ 6% 、 2027 年每股收益增⻓超 15%—— ⽽这两项预测依赖两个正在减弱的⽀点。
其⼀是家庭股票财富。上季度以来已缩⽔约 5 万亿美元，虽全年仍增加约 8 万亿美元，但仅上周
四⼀天标普市值就蒸发约 1.3 万亿美元。其⼆是 AI 资本开⽀：超⼤规模云⼚商 2027 年资本开⽀预
计达 1.3 万亿美元，⾼于今年的 8,220 亿美元和 2025 年的 4,120 亿美元，⽽其合计⾃由现⾦流将从
2025 年的正 1,910 亿美元转为今年的负 610 亿美元，明年进⼀步降⾄负 2,830 亿美元。这⼀缺⼝
必须有⼈融资，这也是过去两周市场紧张的原因：史上最⼤规模 IPO 上市次⽇逼近发⾏价，

SpaceX 设法在不 " 借款 " 的情况下⽀付 470 亿美元购买英伟达芯⽚， Blackstone 旗下数据中⼼公司
未能售出⼀笔 8 亿美元的投资级债券，⽽整个⽣态的 " 最后贷款⼈ " 表⽰将⼤幅提⾼融资⻔槛。
Hartnett 称，选⺠、债券市场与资产配置者发出了同⼀个信号：放慢数据中⼼建设。选⺠在 11 ⽉
3 ⽇表态，配置者在调查中给出了创纪录的 " 过度投资 " ⽐例，债券市场则⾃ 9 ⽉起持续施压。
确认硬着陆的两个指标从未改变：初请失业⾦⼈数超过 25 万，投资级利差超过 100 个基点。若触
发， " 繁荣循环 " （股价涨、财富涨、⽀出涨、资本开⽀涨、股价再涨）将转为 " 末⽇循环 " ，配置
者将被迫减持股票，增持债券、中国资产（财政空间充⾜）、美元，以及与 AI 负相关的资产，如
REITs 与房地产。他的概括是 " 多公寓、空芯⽚ " 。
换⾔之，硬着陆交易就是逆转定义 2020 年代的四⼤交易：除债券什么都买（ 30 年期美债峰⾕价
格回报 -62% ）、除中国什么都买（中国股票本⼗年年均跌 5% ，全球最差）、除美元什么都买（⽐

特币与⻩⾦年复合涨幅分别为 44% 和 23% ，直⾄ 2025 年 10 ⽉和 2026 年 1 ⽉⻅顶）、全⾯押注 AI
（纳斯达克市值⾃ 2023 年 3 ⽉以来增加 26 万亿美元）。其中⼀项已明确逆转，第⼆项⾃ 10 ⽉ 6 ⽇
启动，第三项在债券反弹后开始松动。
集中度⻛险依然存在：⼗⼤ AI 股仍占美国市场的 38% ，与 " 漂亮 50" 的 40% 、⽇本的 44% 和互联⽹
泡沫时期 TMT 的 41% 同处⼀档，远低于铁路时代的 63% 。
2000 年剧本：熊市途中的⼗⼆次反弹
当华尔街多数⼈参照 2018 年时， Hartnett 回到了更早的周期。他写道，市场 5 ⽉还在交易 " 泡
沫 " ，现在已在交易 " 泡沫后 " 。

### Page 5

2000 年的路径是：科技股 3 ⽉⻅顶，美联储当年仍加息 100 个基点，此后市场向 " ⽆聊板块 " 扩散
—— 必需消费、公⽤事业、⾦融与医疗保健上涨 40% ⾄ 60% ，科技股接近腰斩。这与 2026 年夏
季以来的⾛势⾼度相似。尚未出现的是 2001 年：加息追上经济，避⻛港失效， 2000 年的英雄公
⽤事业成为 2001 年表现最差的板块。
两个细节值得注意：美元在 2000 年和 2001 年都很强；纳斯达克在下跌途中出现了⼗⼆次超过
10% 的反弹，其中⼀次 35% 、两次超过 40% 。这对等待宽度规则触发后抢反弹的投资者是必要
的提醒。
第三个细节直接对应 Hartnett 的两个问题：那⼀轮周期最后⼀次加息在 2000 年 5 ⽉，美联储在
2001 年全年降息 475 个基点。 " 加息何时结束 " 的答案是 " 很快 " ；但 " 是否在衰退前结束 " 的答案是
否。 2018 年版本中， Powell 12 ⽉加息、圣诞节前⻅底、 1 ⽉⾸周转向； 2000 年版本中，美联储

提前停⼿，熊市仍因盈利问题延续两年。两者的差距，是仓位事故与资本开⽀崩塌的差距。
资⾦流：股票吸⾦ 666 亿美元，债券⾃ 2025 年 4 ⽉以来⾸次流出
截⾄上周三的⼀周（覆盖英伟达财报与 IPO 前的现⾦筹措，但未覆盖 Anthropic 上市及两个最差
交易⽇），投资者向股票投⼊ 666 亿美元（ ETF 流⼊ 735 亿美元，共同基⾦流出 68 亿美元），现⾦
流⼊ 4 亿美元，⻩⾦流⼊ 2 亿美元；加密货币流出 11 亿美元，债券流出 74 亿美元。
固定收益中，银⾏贷款继续受⻘睐，符合仍相信加息的投资者的配置逻辑；新兴市场债⼀周内
流失超过 1% 的资产。
全年看， 2026 年多项流⼊创纪录：全球股票 1.5 万亿美元（年化）、全球债券 9,090 亿美元、美国
股票 7,510 亿美元、美国⼤盘股 6,690 亿美元、投资级债 4,430 亿美元、美债 2,430 亿美元、科技
基⾦ 2,290 亿美元、韩国 790 亿美元；消费基⾦则创纪录流出 200 亿美元。
现⾦仓位 3.6% 的投资者如何看 2027 年

调查显⽰，基⾦经理预期软着陆、通胀回落、 AI 资本开⽀增速放缓，进⼊ 2027 年时超配股票、
⼤宗商品、新兴市场、医疗保健与⼯业，低配现⾦、债券、 REITs 、消费与能源。最佳资产类别
选择为国际股票（ 36% ）与美国股票（ 24% ），最佳指数为纳斯达克与 MSCI 新兴市场（各
21% ），最佳货币为⽇元（ 42% ）。
其 2027 年底⽬标为： 10 年期美债收益率 4.9% 、欧元兑美元 1.12 、标普 7,926 点、⻩⾦ 4,341 美
元。第⼀项上周五已提前⼗三个⽉实现。标普⽬标较现价⾼ 14% ，⻩⾦⽬标⾼ 24% 。
对逆向投资者⽽⾔， Hartnett 列出了 2026 年有效的配对交易：多原油空加密货币、多能源空可选
消费、多半导体空电信、多⼩盘价值空⼤盘成⻓、多台湾空中国、多⽹络安全空国防。年度主
题则包括：战争优于和平、加息优于降息、贪婪优于恐惧、 AI 发债优于 AI 失业、集中优于宽度，
以及在 1,070 亿美元发⾏的⼀周⾥ IPO 优于回购。若 2027 年这些交易反转，恐惧、宽度与回购将

迎来回归。
80 点之外的悬念
Hartnett ⼗余年来坚持⼀条原则：政策制定者开始恐慌时，市场才会停⽌恐慌。本周的笔记给这
条原则标了价 —— 标普 6,845 点与 IG CDX 80 个基点。前者只需⼀个糟糕的周⼀。
市场已部分替美联储完成了思考： 50 个基点加息被移出、 12 ⽉降息预期消退、 10 年期收益率回
落⾄ 5% 以下。但分歧在于 6,845 点是否是终点。⼀个通胀⽬标连续 67 个⽉未达标、案头摆着三
